#### [8. Awesome Transit](/content/CUTR-at-USF/awesome-transit/README.md)

##### GTFS Converters / Rust

* [GTFS to KML Converter](https://www.getroutepress.com/free-tools/gtfs-to-kml) - Free browser tool that converts GTFS shapes and stops into an organized KML for use in Google Earth.

##### Proprietary (non-standard) vendor APIs / Rust

* [TransCAPI](https://www.transcapi.com/) - REST API for Great Britain bus and rail: departures and arrivals with live running (National Rail Darwin, BODS), live vehicles, disruptions matched to a route, and journey planning. Free tier.

##### GTFS Based Visualizations / Rust

* [À portée de tram](https://tram.camilleroux.com/) - Interactive travel-time maps for 25 cities in France, Belgium and Canada: pick a departure and the city is colored by travel time by tram and metro (optionally bus), with isochrones and a detailed itinerary. Times are computed from GTFS timetables (median ride times, waits from headways, branch-aware waits, published transfer times). Open-source [on GitHub (⭐16)](https://github.com/camilleroux/montpellier-temps-transport) ⭐ 16 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 HTML | 📅 2026-10-12.

##### Agency Tools / General GIS Applications for making transit visualizations

* [Veodyn (⭐13)](https://github.com/veodyn/veodyn-ce) ⭐ 13 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-26 - An open-source, self-hosted data platform for transit agencies: connectors for GTFS, GTFS Realtime, GBFS, TMDD and NTCIP 1203 feeds, historical capture into ClickHouse, a SQL editor, dashboards, a data catalog, and publishing of GBFS and GTFS Realtime feeds. [Documentation](https://docs.veodyn.com) and [live demo](https://demo.veodyn.com).

#### [14. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Agent Orchestration & CLI Tools

* [Bernstein (⭐1.2k)](https://github.com/sipyourdrink-ltd/bernstein) ⭐ 1,229 | 🐛 200 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-27 - Multi-agent orchestrator that coordinates Gemini CLI alongside Claude Code and Codex CLI. Spawns parallel coding agents from a single goal, verifies with tests, auto-commits. Deterministic Python coordination, zero LLM tokens on orchestration.
* [agent-manager (⭐704)](https://github.com/wainmann/agent-manager) ⭐ 713 | 🐛 148 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-11-26 - Terminal UI that runs Gemini CLI alongside Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode and other coding-agent CLIs, each in its own persistent tmux session. One list shows every session's live status, prompts go into a pane without attaching, a session can spawn into its own Git worktree, and a full-file diff review sends line comments back to the agent. Launches your installed Gemini CLI unmodified, so login, config and MCP servers carry over. macOS, Linux and Windows via WSL2. Apache-2.0.
* [Novadeck (⭐59)](https://github.com/novadeck-org/novadeck) ⭐ 64 | 🐛 18 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-27 - Free, open-source desktop app for macOS, Windows and Linux that runs Antigravity CLI (`agy`), Gemini CLI's successor, side by side with Claude Code, Codex and opencode. Every agent's questions and permission asks land in one Inbox, and agents can message each other and start teammates in another harness.
* [Ralph Harness (⭐28)](https://github.com/rxdt/loopgate_harness) ⭐ 28 | 🐛 15 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-12 - Tiny Python scaffold for running Gemini CLI, Claude Code, Codex CLI, and similar coding agents in guarded local loops. It uses repo-local specs, fresh-context iterations, hard caps, git-hook gates, CI verification, and coverage gates.
* [ToutKit (⭐11)](https://github.com/NextProb/nextprob) ⭐ 11 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-24 - Desktop notebook with a built-in terminal that runs Gemini CLI alongside Claude Code and Codex; an in-app webview renders whatever the agent writes inline, and each note is a self-contained folder with its own SQLite, files, and scripts. Local-first, Electron, AGPL-3.0.
* [Jackalope (⭐5)](https://github.com/Jackalope-Dev/jackalope) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-11-26 - Open-source desktop workspace that runs Gemini CLI alongside Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode and other agents, each task in its own git worktree, with diffs, checks and merge review in one view. Windows, macOS and Linux.

##### Commands & Extensions

* [AIHawk (⭐33k)](https://github.com/feder-cr/Jobs_Applier_AI_Agent) ⭐ 32,665 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-24 - Gives Gemini CLI a real Firefox to drive from plain-English instructions: opens pages, clicks, types, reads and screenshots through the real pointer and keyboard. Open source, MIT. Install via `gemini extensions install https://github.com/feder-cr/AIHawk`.
* [llm-box (⭐16)](https://github.com/alib8b8/aflare) ⭐ 16 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-11-03 - Terminal-first workflow automation engine. Generate and execute YAML workflows from plain English. 20+ built-in nodes (fetch\_url, execute, file I/O, HTTP, JSON parsing, template rendering, LLM calls), 15+ LLM providers, and MCP server mode. Install via `gemini extensions install https://github.com/alib8b8/llm-box`.
* [Dopamine Talk (⭐6)](https://github.com/AnnenkovLabs/dopamine-talk) ⭐ 12 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Shell | 📅 2026-11-26 - Agent Skills pack (`dopamine-talk`, `dopamine-changelog`, `dopamine-readme`) and a marker-managed `GEMINI.md` rules block that installs into Gemini CLI (`~/.gemini/skills/` and `~/.gemini/GEMINI.md`) and 20+ other coding agents without touching existing instructions. Cuts file-path narration, trailing hedging disclaimers, boilerplate padding, and unasked critic-gate suggestions. Ships separate native English and Russian editions; zero dependencies, MIT. Install: `curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AnnenkovLabs/dopamine-talk/main/install.sh | sh`.
* [OsmO Phone Calls (⭐11)](https://github.com/getosmo/osmo-ai-plugins) ⭐ 11 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-11-21 - Gemini CLI extension (GEMINI.md context + the remote MCP server `https://api.getosmo.app/mcp`) that has OsmO's voice AI phone businesses or helplines after you confirm, and returns the summary and transcript. OAuth sign-in with a phone number, no API key. 50 free credits for new users in the US, Canada, UK and Australia; paid after. With free credits, OsmO calls businesses and toll-free numbers; calling your contacts and scheduling calls need an OsmO app plan. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/Mountainheads-Private-limited/osmo-ai-plugins`.
* [QuoteMem (⭐7)](https://github.com/edsml-jz2321/QuoteMem) ⭐ 8 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-26 - Gemini CLI extension for long-term memory shared with Claude Code, Codex CLI, Cursor and other agents through one local service: hooks save the chat and add the relevant memories to the context, each memory keeps the exact words it came from, updates keep the old version, and "forget ..." requests are carried out (`npm install -g quotemem`, `quotemem setup`, then `quotemem install gemini`).

* [Nix AI Tools (⭐2.3k)](https://github.com/numtide/llm-agents.nix) ⭐ 2,333 | 🐛 47 | 🌐 Nix | 📅 2026-11-27 - Seamless Nix integration for reproducible Gemini CLI installations. Ensures consistent environments across teams and simplifies deployment with declarative configuration.

* [CloudGPU MCP (⭐2)](https://github.com/cloudgpu-app/cloudgpu-mcp) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 8 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-26 - MIT-licensed local (stdio) MCP server that lets Gemini CLI rent a GPU by the minute on CloudGPU, run a job, download the results and shut the machine down; the same key also works with an OpenAI-compatible API for open models. * [LeadMerlin (⭐0)](https://github.com/HalberdLLC/leadmerlin-mcp) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-26 - MCP server that lets Gemini CLI search local businesses (Google Maps) by niche and city and enrich each one with owner name, emails checked against the mail server, phone line type (mobile/landline/VoIP) and website tech. MIT.
* [A1 Gallery](https://www.a1.gallery/mcp?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Hosted MCP server that lets Gemini CLI search a hand-picked gallery of real, live websites by style, font, colour and section, and read the type sizes, spacing and palette measured off each page. Remote Streamable HTTP with OAuth and a free A1 account: `gemini mcp add --transport http a1 https://www.a1.gallery/api/mcp`.

* :free: [SpriteForge Pixel Art Maker](https://www.spriteforge.tech/pixel-art-maker) - Free browser pixel art editor with layers, animation frames and onion skin, Aseprite-style keys, and PNG, GIF, sprite sheet or .aseprite export; no account.

##### Collections / Voxel Editors

* :money\_with\_wings: [RFTracks](https://rftracks.com/use-cases/gaming) - Royalty free music for games made with AI tools, filterable by mood, genre and tempo, MP3 downloads; free for commercial games with a credit link to RFTracks, paid plans without credit.

#### [18. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### Code Quality

* [CodePeel](https://www.codepeel.com) - Automated code reviews for GitHub pull requests that catch bugs and open fix PRs. Free forever for unlimited public and private repositories (30 reviews/month).

##### CI and CD

* [BuildPulse](https://buildpulse.io/products/runners) - Faster runners for GitHub Actions, CircleCI and Semaphore. 3,000 free minutes every month, no credit card required.

##### Monitoring

* [12happy](https://www.12hpy.com/) - Free uptime service monitoring with status page for your product. Apps for both iOS and Android, notifications via Slack, Telegram and E-mail. Various locations. Free plan includes 50 monitors checked every 5 minutes.
* [Pingaroo](https://pingaroo.dev/) - 1 website, 2 URLs, uptime check with 15 minutes interval, SSL/DNS/domain checks, email alerts, no credit card needed.

##### Education and Career Development

* [SharpSkill](https://sharpskill.dev/) - Free tier of a technical interview preparation platform including cheatsheets, flashcards, tests and code review exercises in 20 technologies, plus public job market data by technology and country.
* [TCM Academy Free Tier](https://tcm-sec.com/academy/tcm-academy-free-tier/) - Free, full six course tier with over 40 hours of training for IT beginners.
